{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"supplies","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"supplying","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"supplied","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"supplied","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"to supply money for the war","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To provide (something), to make (something) available for use."],"synonyms":[{"word":"fulfill"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to supply a furnace with fuel; to supply soldiers with ammunition","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To furnish or equip with."],"synonyms":[{"word":"line"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Rivers are supplied by smaller streams.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To fill up, or keep full."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To compensate for, or make up a deficiency of."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To serve instead of; to take the place of."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To act as a substitute."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to supply a pulpit","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To fill temporarily; to serve as substitute for another in, as a vacant place or office; to occupy; to have possession of."],"tags":["transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"səplīʹ"},{"ipa":"/səˈplaɪ/"},{"ipa":"[sʌˈpʰɫaɪ]"}],"synonyms":[{"word":"provision"}],"upstream_line":258318,"upstream_offset":1285564329,"upstream_record_sha256":"5a30836047aba5af79f32e255491ed2e08c37b15fff97d8e36a54fc4aae09e30","word":"supply"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"supplies","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["The act of supplying."],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"A supply of good drinking water is essential.","type":"example"},{"text":"She said, \"China has always had a freshwater supply problem with 20 percent of the world’s population but only 7 percent of its freshwater\".","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An amount of something supplied."],"tags":["countable"]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"demand"}],"examples":[{"text":"Supply and demand ebb and flow in a complex interplay.","type":"example"},{"text":"The supply of timber to the region's mills was lower than expected this month, owing to transport problems posed by wildfires.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The market force that causes sellers to be both willing and able to sell a good or service, as measured by the amount of that good or service that is currently available to be bought at any given price point; the amount itself."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["economics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"They are busy laying in supplies for the coming winter.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Provisions."],"tags":["countable","in-plural","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to vote supplies","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An amount of money provided, as by Parliament or Congress, to meet the annual national expenditures."],"tags":["countable","in-plural","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Somebody, such as a teacher or clergyman, who temporarily fills the place of another; a substitute."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"səplīʹ"},{"ipa":"/səˈplaɪ/"},{"ipa":"[sʌˈpʰɫaɪ]"}],"upstream_line":258319,"upstream_offset":1285603714,"upstream_record_sha256":"159df473dddd6dadb10b888199febf38a764caa27c532bb60f7a00d0b9daedcc","word":"supply"}
